- **act**: Use when a settled answer needs to become real action and the user wants the steps put into motion through the tools they already have ("make it happen", "set this up", "do it", "get this going", "book it"). - **act-aloha**: S1 Vision-Language-Action policy. Capabilities: transfer, pick, place on cube. Action Chunking Transformer (~52M-param encoder-decoder) finetuned on the ALOHA bimanual cube-transfer demonstration set. Action chunks of length 100. The mean/std norm buffers live inside model.safetensors; modern lerobot ACTPolicy drops them on load and the adapter re-applies them — see tests/sim/test_aloha_bimanual_act_aloha.py. Discovery view of an OpenRAL rSkill — NOT directly runnable by an agent harness; it runs via rSkill.from_pretrained + the robot HAL. ([OpenRAL/openral](https://github.com/OpenRAL/openral/tree/HEAD/rskills/act-aloha) · ★69 · 2 copies)
- **act-aloha-insertion**: S1 Vision-Language-Action policy. Capabilities: insert, pick, place on peg, socket. ACT (~52M params, chunk=100) finetuned on the ALOHA bimanual sim-insertion demonstration set. Insertion is the harder ALOHA task in the original paper; the 0.20 success rate reflects that. See the norm-stats note above re: the legacy safetensors-resident buffers. Discovery view of an OpenRAL rSkill — NOT directly runnable by an agent harness; it runs via rSkill.from_pretrained + the robot HAL. ([OpenRAL/openral](https://github.com/OpenRAL/openral/tree/HEAD/rskills/act-aloha-insertion) · ★69 · 2 copies)

([wrufeger/agentic-coding-template](https://github.com/wrufeger/agentic-coding-template/tree/HEAD/.act/skills/act-test-gap) · ★9)
- **act-test-phase**: Run the Test phase of the ACT process -- implement the consented proposal on a reversible, time-limited basis with defined success criteria, midpoint check-in, and structured review leading to adoption, extension, modification, or revert. ([Community-Chests/neos-operating-system](https://github.com/Community-Chests/neos-operating-system/tree/HEAD/neos-core/layer-03-act-engine/act-test-phase))
- **act-testing**: Prepares act-testable GitHub Actions workflow scenarios for this monorepo. ([camunda/camunda](https://github.com/camunda/camunda/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/act-testing) · ★4.4k)
- **act-transition**: 현재 막에서 다음 막으로 전환 가능한지 campaign.yaml 의 act_gates 조건식을 평가. 매 씬 말미·주요 비트 후 호출. ([icepeng/agentchan](https://github.com/icepeng/agentchan/tree/HEAD/example_data/library/templates/three-winds-ledger/skills/act-transition) · ★5)
- **act-true-site-size-sandbox**: Run the true-site-size GitHub Action locally via `act` inside the Claude Code web sandbox, to measure the game/editor cold-load journey byte cost without a CI round-trip. Use when asked to measure or verify build/wire size, or to check a byte-saving change, in the sandbox. ([jimhigson/head-over-heels-online](https://github.com/jimhigson/head-over-heels-online/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/act-true-site-size-sandbox) · ★16)
- **act-update**: Use to check for or apply a newer template version, or when asked which template version is installed or newest ("update the template", "act-update"). ([wrufeger/agentic-coding-template](https://github.com/wrufeger/agentic-coding-template/tree/HEAD/.act/skills/act-update) · ★9)
- **act-version**: Use when asked which template version is installed or which releases the source offers ("act-version", "is there a newer release"). Writes only the local update note. - **act-vscode-workspace-bootstrap**: Adds approved merge-safe GitHub, Azure DevOps, editor, and Scout .vscode scaffolding to a selected workspace. - **act-when-ready**: Stop Claude Fable 5 / 5.1 from over-planning, re-deriving settled facts, or surveying options it will never pursue. Use in any interactive or agentic session where responses feel slow, turns run long on simple asks, or the model keeps restating context before acting. Especially valuable at high effort settings and in ambiguous, multi-threaded requests. ([kpab/claude-fable-5-skills](https://github.com/kpab/claude-fable-5-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/act-when-ready) · ★19 · 4 copies)
- **act-with-the-person**: Work skill. A task runs with the person's hands and yours together — a connection, a sign-in, DNS records, a Telegram bot, a publication, a comment, a payment page, any step in a service where some acts are yours (the Claude in Chrome extension, the node's commands) and some only theirs (a password, a code, a sign-in, a final «publish» or «pay», a choice). Load it before the first step of such a task and whenever you are about to ask the person to do something with their hands: their act is a window at the bottom of the panel with «Готово» (npm run ask-person), never a line in the chat. ([Fractera/web4-node-starter-kit](https://github.com/Fractera/web4-node-starter-kit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/act-with-the-person) · ★69)
- **act-within-scope**: Use when deciding whether to ask the user a question, make an execution choice, or continue an in-progress task. Resolve ordinary, reversible decisions that are within scope from the request and repository evidence. Ask only when intent, authority, scope, cost, or external impact is genuinely uncertain. ([AnthonyPoschen/agent-skills](https://github.com/AnthonyPoschen/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/act-within-scope) · ★2)

- **acta**: Pubblica e scopri sulla bacheca condivisa della federazione Acta (modello «pubblicare, non spedire»). Triggers: 'pubblica su Acta', 'affiggi sulla bacheca', 'pubblica l'esito X su Acta', 'scopri sulla bacheca', 'controlla la bacheca Acta', 'novità dagli altri nodi', 'cosa c'è nel canale Y', 'check the Acta board', 'what's new on the board', 'did another node publish X'. - **acta**: Guide developers through integrating acta.build's verifiable credentials infrastructure on Stellar blockchain.
